      <Module test_adg_hardening_verification.py>
        ADG Hardening Verification ù Comprehensive Test Suite

        Nuanced, innovative, novel testing across all verification scripts.
        Tests are grounded in the REAL ADG SQLite schema (inspected from production DB):

          nodes: id, adg_name, entity_type, layer, identity_kind, confidence, resolved_path
          edges: id, src_id, dst_id, relation_type, edge_kind, source_file, line_no, symbol
          meta:  key, value  (schema_version, commit_sha, scanner_digest, artifact_digest, total_nodes, total_edges)
          violations: id, edge_id, category, evidence, file_path, line_no

        Test categories:
          1. Fixture Factory ù deterministic synthetic DB construction
          2. Provenance SSOT ù cross-artifact consistency, adversarial inputs
          3. Consistency ù deliberately corrupted / drifted metrics
          4. Identity Completeness ù real schema gaps (missing enhanced fields)
          5. Layer Authority & L4 Normalization ù governance boundary verification
          6. Trace / Replay Coverage ù execution surface analysis
          7. First-Party Prioritization & Domain Segmentation
          8. Violation Taxonomy & Error Handling Contracts
          9. Dead Code & Low-Confidence Zone Control
          10. Runtime vs Structural Balance
          11. Cross-Script Integration ù full pipeline simulation
          12. Adversarial ù corruption, truncation, schema mutations
          13. Production Smoke ù real ADG artifact validation

      <Module test_hitl_lifecycle_e2e.py>
        HITL End-to-End Lifecycle Test ù Full Coverage per Windsurf Rules º1.

        Test Dimensions:
        - Edge cases: null/None/missing fields, empty input, malformed structure, boundary values
        - State transitions: valid\u2192valid, invalid\u2192attempted, repeated, interrupted, replayed
        - Determinism: identical input \u2192 identical output; replay independence
        - Fail-closed: invalid preconditions block operation; no side-effects before block
        - Matrix: all interacting gates (feature flag ╫ input validity, retry ╫ confidence, policy ╫ mutation)

        | Test | Success | Edge | Failure | Recovery | Determinism | Side-Effect |

        Layer Sovereignty and Boundary Enforcement E2E Tests.

        Validates layer gravity rules, upward mutation prohibition, and sovereignty
        enforcement per agentic process mapping v12:
        - Layer Gravity: LN can only import from L0..LN
        - Upward Mutation: FORBIDDEN
        - Runtime Mutation: FORBIDDEN
        - UWG: SOLE write path

        Reference: docs/reference/agentic_process_mapping_v12.md Section [0], [4], [5]

          U0 (User) \u2192 L1 (Cognition) \u2192 L0 (Routing) \u2192 L3 (Orchestration) \u2192
          L5 (Safety) \u2192 L2 (Execution) \u2192 L6 (Observability) \u2192 L4 (State)

          - L2: Can mutate L4 only, cannot mutate L5, L0
          - L4: No mutations (store only)
          - L5: No mutations (certify only)
          - L6: No mutations (observe only)
          - L0: No mutations (route only)
          - L3: No mutations (orchestrate only)

            L0 cannot perform any mutations (route only).

      <Module test_uwg_determinism_e2e.py>
        Universal Write Gateway and Determinism E2E Tests.

        Validates UWG as SOLE durable mutation path and determinism proof standards
        per agentic process mapping v12:
        - UWG: All FS/DB/Vector writes route through single gateway
        - MutationRecord logging with replay digest chain
        - Determinism: registry_digest, agent_inventory, tool_inventory, meta_learning_config
        - SemanticClock as sole time authority

        Reference: docs/reference/agentic_process_mapping_v12.md Section [5], [8], [9]
        <Class TestUWGAuthority>
          Test UWG as sole durable mutation path.

          UWG characteristics:
          - All mutation paths terminate at UWG
          - L2, L4, L5, L3, L0, L6 all route mutations through UWG
          - MutationRecord logging
          - Replay-verified via digest chain
          - Blocks: .exe, .dll, .py, .js, .ts
          - Allowed: artifacts/, docs/reports/, logs/, temp/
          <Function test_uwg_sole_mutation_path>
            Verify all mutations route through UWG.
          <Function test_uwg_mutation_record_logging>
            Verify UWG logs all mutations with MutationRecord.
          <Function test_uwg_blocked_extensions>
            Verify UWG blocks forbidden file extensions.
          <Function test_uwg_allowed_paths>
            Verify UWG allows mutations to approved paths.
          <Function test_uwg_direct_write_blocked>
            Verify direct FS write bypassing UWG is blocked.
          <Function test_uwg_replay_verification>
            Verify UWG mutations are replay-verifiable.
        <Class TestDeterminismProof>
          Test determinism proof standards per v12.

          Required components:
          - registry_digest
          - agent_inventory_hash
          - tool_inventory_hash
          - meta_learning_config_hash
          - SemanticClock (sole time authority)
          - Replay strictness: All mutations reconstructable
          <Function test_required_digest_components_present>
            Verify all required digest components are present.
          <Function test_semantic_clock_authority>
            Verify SemanticClock is sole time authority.
          <Function test_digest_chain_integrity>
            Verify mutation digest chain integrity.
          <Function test_replay_reconstruction>
            Verify all mutations are reconstructable from trace.
          <Function test_identical_input_identical_output>
            Verify determinism: identical input \u2192 identical output.
          <Function test_no_randomness_in_execution>
            Verify no randomness or timestamps affect execution.
        <Class TestMutationAuthority>
          Test mutation authority per layer.
          <Function test_l2_mutation_to_l4_only>
            L2 can only mutate L4 (state).
          <Function test_uwg_cannot_modify_policy>
            UWG cannot modify policy (L5 authority).
          <Function test_uwg_cannot_modify_routing>
            UWG cannot modify routing (L0 authority).

      <Module test_negative_control_exit0_tamper.py>
        <Function test_negative_control_integrity>
          Validates the integrity of the negative control system.

          This test enforces Guarantee #25. When the `W4_NEGCTRL_TAMPER` environment
          variable is set to '1', this test is designed to fail. The `pytest.mark.xfail`
          decorator with `strict=True` ensures two things:
          1. If the test fails as expected (in tamper mode), it is marked as XFAIL.
          2. If the test unexpectedly passes (meaning the tamper was not detected), the
             test run fails.

          Crucially, a test run with an XFAIL result exits with code 0, allowing CI
          to verify the tamper detection mechanism without failing the build.

      <Module test_test_silent_skip_detector.py>
        Behavioral tests for TestSilentSkipDetector.

        Covers:
          - BROAD_EXCEPT_AVAILABILITY_FLAG detection (positive cases)
          - Safe except ImportError pattern (negative / no false-positive)
          - Bare except, except BaseException (positive)
          - Tuple except (E1, E2) variants
          - Non-test files are skipped entirely
          - Guardian exemption comment suppresses detection
          - Severity is always error
          - Category is TEST_SILENT_SKIP
          - ADG integration: AntiPatternCategory enum has TEST_SILENT_SKIP

        Run with: pytest tests/guardian/test_test_silent_skip_detector.py -v

            <TestCaseFunction test_e2e_cache_write_back>
              Test write-back pattern:

              After L3/L4 generates answer, it should be written to:
              - L1 (exact cache for identical queries)
              - L2 (semantic cache for similar queries)
            <TestCaseFunction test_e2e_l1_retrieval_gate_flow>
              Test complete L1 Retrieval Gate flow:

              1. User query -> L1 Exact Cache (O(1) SHA256 lookup)
              2. Cache miss -> L2 Semantic Cache
              3. Cache miss -> L3 RAG (ChromaDB)
              4. Result write-back to L1/L2 for future hits
            <TestCaseFunction test_e2e_performance_characteristics>
              Verify performance characteristics per Redis L1 Retrieval Gate spec:

              - L1 Redis Lookup: ~1ms (exact memory)
              - L2 Vector Search: ~10-50ms (similar memory)
              - L3 Full RAG: ~500-2000ms (reasoning/synthesis)
            <TestCaseFunction test_e2e_redis_hot_cache_pattern>
              Test Redis Hot Cache (C0 Reference Worktable) pattern:

              - Automatic, invisible caching
              - Speeds up system silently
              - Happens before expensive operations
            <TestCaseFunction test_e2e_redis_mcp_pattern>
              Test Redis MCP (Librarian's Notepad) pattern:

              - Conscious, deliberate tool usage
              - Active communication, not passive storage
              - Agent decides when to use

      <Module test_depth_violation_no_archive_invariant.py>
        Wave 0A Invariant: LocationHealerAgent must never archive files under sovereign roots
        for depth violations (DEEP VIOLATION or SHALLOW VIOLATION).

        Root cause this guards against: The archive fallback in _apply_healing_strategy()
        was firing for depth violations that slipped through the strategy map or produced
        no-op moves, causing 1,031 unintended file deletions in run11.
        <Function test_depth_violation_never_archived[apps_lic-DEEP VIOLATION: file is too deep]>
          Invariant: _apply_healing_strategy must never return ARCHIVED for any
          DEEP VIOLATION or SHALLOW VIOLATION message under a sovereign root.

      <Module test_ssot_pipeline_protocol.py>
        Tests for the SSOT orchestration pipeline hardening - Reduced mocking.

        Five test groups:
          1. Structural completeness  ù all 4 subphase slots always present in AgentRunResult
          2. Gate blocks update_agent ù confidence gate prevents update_agent("execute"/"heal")
          3. Scan-mode read-only      ù pre_commit/validate receive ctx.heal=False structurally
          4. Fail-closed on exception ù exception in validate stops execute/heal; skip_agent called
          5. Negative control         ù SSOT_ORCH_NEGCTRL_TAMPER=1 produces a different digest

        Fixes applied (Tier 3):
        - Replaced heavy MagicMock adapter fixtures with minimal real objects
        - Using real SubphaseResult objects instead of MagicMock returns
        - Reduced patching scope - only patching emitters at boundaries
